+### Expansive Depth Mode
+
+- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of follow-up questions beside the seeded key questions. An absent flag means `default`, which renders the same bytes as before.
+- `step_frontier_seed` writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed`, after `step_create_config`, or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run.
+- `step_frontier_admit` runs before `step_reduce_state` and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Admitted nodes land in `research/inbox.jsonl` with `origin: "frontier"`.

+- After the `minIterations` floor an open or active node turns a soft stop into CONTINUE with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never creates a stop and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`.
+- A fan-out run deals the seeded depth-1 nodes round-robin across lineages and merges them back under a partition guard, so no dealt node goes unreported.

 ### Invocation Contract
 
 This skill is invoked exclusively through `/deep:research:auto` or `/deep:research:confirm` -- the command YAML owns state, dispatch, convergence, and synthesis. Never simulate the loop with ad hoc shell dispatch, nested CLI loops, direct `@deep-research` Task dispatch, `/tmp` prompt files, or state outside the resolved local research packet.

 `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` computes the whole stop decision once per pass from the config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. `deep-review.yaml` renders no stop arithmetic of its own. The frozen stop reasons are `converged`, `maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `manualStop`, `error`, `stuckRecovery`, `userPaused` and `maxDurationReached`. A missing graph decision blocks every non-terminal STOP.
 
 The stuck count and the consecutive failure count are rebuilt from the state log on every pass, so a resumed run counts the same as one that never paused. Failed iterations stay out of the ratio signals. Three trailing failed iterations stop the loop with stop reason `error`. In confirm mode only, the workflow first asks the operator Retry, Skip or Cancel. Auto mode has no such question.
 
 Each pass also compares `maxDurationMinutes` with the config `createdAt`. When the limit is reached the loop skips the dispatch and goes to synthesis with stop reason `maxDurationReached`.
 
+### Expansive Depth Mode
+
+- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.
+- The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, set to `maxIterations` at config creation. Both are locked against the optimizer.
+- `step_frontier_seed` writes the depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --target review`, after `step_create_config` or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run. A merge with no diff of its own is pruned `merge-no-own-diff` and an index rebuild `generated-artifact`.
+- `step_frontier_admit` runs before the reducer and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, written only by `frontier-admit.cjs`; review has no inbox.
+- `step_read_state` reads the active node and sets `next_dimension` to its next uncovered dimension, `step_frontier_activate` marks it active before dispatch, and the prompt pack's `{frontier_block}` names the node. Next Focus takes the blocked stop, then the pivot, then the node, then the leaf focus, then the first uncovered dimension.
+- While a node stays open the frontier veto turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the review floor, and stuck recovery closes the active node `exhausted`.
+- A fan-out run deals the depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order, binds `frontier_scope:` beside `review_focus:`, records the partition as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json` and returns a partition guard for any node no lineage reports. Size `--max-iterations` so half of it covers `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`.

[0m$ [0mcd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop && sed -n '690,800p' runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs
throw new Error(`cannot read ${label} ${filePath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);

}

function readKnownTexts(filePath) {
  const parsed = readJsonFile(filePath, 'known-texts file');
  // A malformed known-texts file would silently widen admission, so it is an
  // error rather than a skipped source.
  if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error('--known-texts must hold a JSON array');
  return parsed;
}

function readSeedCandidates(filePath) {
  const parsed = readJsonFile(filePath, 'input file');
  if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error('--input for seed must hold a JSON array of candidates');
  return parsed;
}

function readProposalRows(filePath) {
  let content;
  try {
    content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(`cannot read input file ${filePath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  }

  const proposals = [];
  for (const line of content.split('\n')) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (!trimmed) continue;
    let row;
    try {
      row = JSON.parse(trimmed);
    } catch {
      continue;
    }
    if (!isPlainObject(row) || row.type !== 'frontier_proposal') continue;
    proposals.push(isPlainObject(row.proposal) ? row.proposal : row);
  }
  return proposals;
}

function runCli(argv) {
  const args = parseArgs(argv);
  const command = args.command;

  if (command !== 'seed' && command !== 'admit' && command !== 'close'
    && command !== 'graph' && command !== 'block') {
    throw new Error(`unknown command: ${command === null ? '(none)' : command}; expected seed, admit, close, graph or block`);
  }

  const artifactDir = requireFlag(args, 'artifactDir', 'artifact-dir');
  const target = assertTarget(args.target === undefined ? 'research' : args.target);

  if (command === 'graph') {
    const output = requireFlag(args, 'output', 'output');
    return writeFrontierGraph({ artifactDir, output });
  }

  // The block is prompt text, so it goes to stdout verbatim rather than through
  // the JSON envelope the record-shaped commands return.
  if (command === 'block') {
    // A review leaf carries its node id, while the research block recovers the
    // node from the focus string the reducer wrote.
    const blockOptions = target === 'review'
      ? { artifactDir, nodeId: requireFlag(args, 'nodeId', 'node-id'), dimension: args.dimension, target }
      : { artifactDir, focus: requireFlag(args, 'focus', 'focus'), target };
    process.stdout.write(renderFrontierBlock(blockOptions));
    return undefined;
  }

  const iteration = parseIterationArg(args.iteration);
  const knownTexts = args.knownTexts === undefined ? [] : readKnownTexts(args.knownTexts);

  if (command === 'seed') {
    const candidates = readSeedCandidates(requireFlag(args, 'input', 'input'));
    return seedNodes({ artifactDir, iteration, candidates, knownTexts, target });
  }

  if (command === 'admit') {
    const proposals = readProposalRows(requireFlag(args, 'input', 'input'));
    return admitProposals({ artifactDir, iteration, proposals, knownTexts, target });
  }

  const nodeId = requireFlag(args, 'nodeId', 'node-id');
  const status = requireFlag(args, 'status', 'status');
  return closeNode({ artifactDir, nodeId, status, reason: args.reason === undefined ? null : args.reason, iteration });
}

function main(argv = process.argv.slice(2)) {
  try {
    const result = runCli(argv);
    if (result !== undefined) process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(result)}\n`);
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (error) {
    process.stderr.write(`frontier-admit: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
    process.exit(2);
  }
}

if (require.main === module) {
  main();
}

function seedNodes({ artifactDir, iteration = 0, candidates, knownTexts = [], target = 'research' }) {
  // Every seeded node sits at depth 1 against the run's synthetic root: the seed
  // sees only the topic, so it cannot justify a deeper placement on its own.
  // A run is seeded once. A re-run of the seed step (a restart that kept the
  // store, a repeated fan-out launch) would otherwise add a reworded second
  // decomposition beside the first, and a fan-out partition would then name
  // nodes no lineage holds.
  const mode = assertTarget(target);
  const storePath = path.join(artifactDir, NODE_STORE_FILE);
  const existing = readNodeStore(storePath);
  if ([...existing.values()].some((node) => node.depth === 1)) {
    return { admitted: [], pruned: [], skipped: 'already-seeded' };
  }

  const pruned = [];
  const proposals = [];

  for (const candidate of Array.isArray(candidates) ? candidates : []) {
    const pruneReason = mode === 'review' && isPlainObject(candidate) ? candidate.pruneReason : undefined;
    if (!isNonEmptyString(pruneReason)) {
      proposals.push(isPlainObject(candidate) ? { ...candidate, parentId: 'root' } : candidate);
      continue;
    }

    // A reviewed sub-target can arrive already settled: a merged duplicate, a
    // generated artifact, a file with no diff of its own. The seed knows that
    // before any proposal exists, so the row is pruned here rather than through
    // an admission check that would have to reach the same conclusion.
    const nodeId = `F-1-${String(existing.size + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`;
    const row = {
      nodeId,
      parentId: 'root',
      depth: 1,
      scope: candidate.scope === undefined ? null : candidate.scope,
      question: candidate.question === undefined ? null : candidate.question,
      status: 'pruned',
      reason: pruneReason,
      iteration: normalizeIteration(iteration),
      dedupeKey: dedupeKey(candidate.question),
    };
    appendJsonlRow(storePath, row);
    existing.set(nodeId, row);
    pruned.push({ nodeId, reason: pruneReason });
  }

  const result = admitProposals({ artifactDir, iteration, proposals, knownTexts, target: mode });

  return { admitted: result.admitted, pruned: [...pruned, ...result.pruned] };
}

function closeNode({ artifactDir, nodeId, status, reason = null, iteration }) {
  if (!CLOSE_STATUSES.has(status)) {
    throw new Error(`unknown close status: ${status}; expected one of ${[...CLOSE_STATUSES].join(', ')}`);
  }

  const storePath = path.join(artifactDir, NODE_STORE_FILE);
  const nodes = readNodeStore(storePath);
  const node = nodes.get(nodeId);
  if (!node) throw new Error(`unknown frontier node: ${nodeId}`);

  const row = {
    ...node,
    status,
    reason,
    iteration: normalizeIteration(iteration),
  };
  appendJsonlRow(storePath, row);
  return row;
}

// The block is computed, not written by the orchestrating model, so the slot count
// the leaf sees is the one admission will enforce.
function renderFrontierBlock({ artifactDir, focus, nodeId, dimension, target = 'research' }) {
  const mode = assertTarget(target);
  let resolvedId;

  if (mode === 'review') {
    // A review leaf is dispatched to one node, so its id is handed in whole and
    // there is no focus string to parse back into one.
    resolvedId = typeof nodeId === 'string' ? nodeId : '';
  } else {
    if (typeof focus !== 'string' || focus === '') return '';

    const match = /^Frontier node (\S+) /.exec(focus);
    if (!match) return '';

    resolvedId = match[1];
  }

  const nodes = readNodeStore(path.join(artifactDir, NODE_STORE_FILE));
  const node = nodes.get(resolvedId);
  if (!node) return '';
  // A closed node's block would invite proposals admission can only prune.
  if (node.status !== 'open' && node.status !== 'active') return '';

  const caps = readCaps(artifactDir, mode);
  const depth = Number.isFinite(node.depth) ? node.depth : 0;
  const scope = Array.isArray(node.scope) ? node.scope.join(', ') : (node.scope || 'unscoped');

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const NODE_STORE_FILE = 'frontier-nodes.jsonl';
const INBOX_FILE = 'inbox.jsonl';
const CONFIG_FILE = 'deep-research-config.json';
const REVIEW_CONFIG_FILE = 'deep-review-config.json';

// A target names the loop a call speaks for: which config file it reads and
// whether an admitted node queues a question.
const TARGETS = Object.freeze(['research', 'review']);

const DEFAULT_MAX_DEPTH = 3;
const DEFAULT_MAX_CHILDREN_PER_NODE = 6;
const DEFAULT_MAX_FRONTIER_NODES = 10;

// The seed step decomposes a target into a documented number of depth-1 nodes:
// 3 to 6 sub-questions for research, 3 to 8 sub-targets for review. The root's
// children are those nodes, so its cap is the top of that range, never below the
// per-node cap. A fan-out parent seeds before any config exists, so this cannot
// come from config alone.
const ROOT_CHILDREN_FLOOR = Object.freeze({ research: 6, review: 8 });

const NODE_STATUSES = Object.freeze([
  'open',
  'active',
  'answered',
  'blocked',
  'exhausted',
  'abstained',
  'pruned',
]);

// close cannot move a node back to the unopened or admitted state: seeding and
// admission own those, and a re-close is only meaningful for a lifecycle move.
const CLOSE_STATUSES = new Set([
  'active',
  'answered',
  'blocked',
  'exhausted',
  'abstained',
  'pruned',
]);

// A node closed with one of these never answered its question: the branch ran
// out, or it was dropped. The graph projection has to keep that visible.
const UNANSWERED_CLOSE_STATUSES = new Set([
  'blocked',
  'exhausted',
  'abstained',
]);

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. HELPERS
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function isPlainObject(value) {
  return Boolean(value) && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function isNonEmptyString(value) {
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== '';
}

function hasScope(scope) {
  if (typeof scope === 'string') return scope.trim() !== '';
  return Array.isArray(scope) && scope.length > 0;
}

function normalizeIteration(value) {
  return Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 ? value : 0;
}

function capValue(value, fallback) {
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0 ? Math.floor(value) : fallback;
}

function assertTarget(target) {
  if (!TARGETS.includes(target)) {
    throw new Error(`unknown target: ${String(target)}; expected ${TARGETS.join(' or ')}`);
  }
  return target;
}

function appendJsonlRow(filePath, row) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.appendFileSync(filePath, `${JSON.stringify(row)}\n`, 'utf8');
}

function nodeDedupeKey(node) {
  return typeof node.dedupeKey === 'string' ? node.dedupeKey : dedupeKey(node.question);
}

function depthFor(proposal, nodes) {
  const parentId = isPlainObject(proposal) ? proposal.parentId : null;
  if (parentId === 'root') return 1;
  const parent = nodes.get(parentId);
  // A node with no resolvable parent was never placed on the graph. Zero keeps
  // that fact visible instead of inventing a depth the node never had.
  return parent && Number.isFinite(parent.depth) ? parent.depth + 1 : 0;
}

// Question coverage and source diversity divide by every QUESTION node, so a
// node that closed unanswered would hold both signals down for the rest of the
// run. It is re-kinded to a CLAIM marked closed instead: CLAIM feeds only the
// claim-verification rate, which counts a closed claim as settled.
function buildFrontierGraphEvents(nodes) {
  const rows = Array.from(nodes ?? [])
    .filter((row) => isPlainObject(row) && isNonEmptyString(row.nodeId))
    .sort((left, right) => (left.nodeId < right.nodeId ? -1 : left.nodeId > right.nodeId ? 1 : 0));

  const written = new Set();
  const nodeEvents = [];
  const edgeEvents = [];

  for (const row of rows) {
    // A pruned proposal never entered the run, so it never reaches the graph.
    if (row.status === 'pruned') continue;

    const baseMetadata = {
      frontierNodeId: row.nodeId,
      depth: row.depth,
      scope: row.scope,
      status: row.status,
    };
    const closed = UNANSWERED_CLOSE_STATUSES.has(row.status);

    nodeEvents.push({
      type: 'node',
      id: `frontier-${row.nodeId}`,
      kind: closed ? 'CLAIM' : 'QUESTION',
      name: row.question,
      iteration: normalizeIteration(row.iteration),
      metadata: closed
        ? { ...baseMetadata, reason: row.reason, verification_status: 'closed' }
        : baseMetadata,
    });
    written.add(row.nodeId);
  }

  for (const row of rows) {
    if (!written.has(row.nodeId)) continue;
    if (row.parentId === 'root' || !written.has(row.parentId)) continue;
    edgeEvents.push({
      type: 'edge',
      id: `frontier-${row.nodeId}-derived-from-${row.parentId}`,
      sourceId: `frontier-${row.nodeId}`,
      targetId: `frontier-${row.parentId}`,
      relation: 'DERIVED_FROM',
    });
  }

try { row = JSON.parse(line); } catch { continue; }

    byId.set(row.nodeId, row);
  }
  return [...byId.values()].sort((left, right) => left.nodeId.localeCompare(right.nodeId));
}

const FRONTIER_STATUSES = ['open', 'active', 'answered', 'blocked', 'exhausted', 'abstained', 'pruned'];

// A node the store still holds open is answered once its question resolves in
// the registry, so the status is derived here instead of written back.
function buildFrontierProjection(frontierInput, keyedQuestions) {
  const resolvedByNode = new Map();
  for (const question of keyedQuestions) {
    if (question.inboxId) resolvedByNode.set(question.inboxId, question.resolved);
  }
  const nodes = frontierInput.nodes.map((node) => {
    const stillOpen = node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active';
    const status = stillOpen && resolvedByNode.get(node.nodeId) === true ? 'answered' : node.status;
    return {
      nodeId: node.nodeId,
      parentId: node.parentId ?? null,
      depth: node.depth,
      scope: node.scope ?? null,
      question: node.question ?? null,
      status,
      reason: status === node.status ? (node.reason ?? null) : null,
      iteration: node.iteration ?? null,
    };
  });
  const stats = { total: nodes.length };
  for (const status of FRONTIER_STATUSES) stats[status] = nodes.filter((node) => node.status === status).length;
  return { nodes, stats, ...(frontierInput.budget ? { budget: frontierInput.budget } : {}) };
}

// The frontier focus names the node and never repeats its question text: a
// question resolves when an iteration's focus equals its text, so echoing the
// text would close a node just by visiting it.
// The leaf writes its own focus line and may reword it, so the node marker counts
// wherever it appears; the id must end there, so F-1-001 never matches F-1-0010.
function frontierNodeVisits(nodeId, iterationRecords) {
  const escaped = String(nodeId).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
  const marker = new RegExp(`(?:^|[^\\w-])Frontier node ${escaped}(?![\\w-])`);
  return (Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords : [])
    .filter((record) => typeof record?.focus === 'string' && marker.test(record.focus))
    .length;
}

function pickFrontierFocus(frontier, iterationRecords) {
  const open = (Array.isArray(frontier?.nodes) ? frontier.nodes : [])
    .filter((node) => node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active');
  if (open.length === 0) return '';
  // Breadth first: the shallowest node, then one not yet visited, then the oldest.
  const ranked = open
    .map((node) => ({ node, visits: frontierNodeVisits(node.nodeId, iterationRecords) }))
    .sort((left, right) => (left.node.depth - right.node.depth)
      || (left.visits - right.visits)
      || ((left.node.iteration ?? 0) - (right.node.iteration ?? 0))
      || left.node.nodeId.localeCompare(right.node.nodeId));
  const chosen = ranked[0].node;
  const scope = Array.isArray(chosen.scope) ? chosen.scope.join(', ') : (chosen.scope || 'unscoped');
  return `Frontier node ${chosen.nodeId} (depth ${chosen.depth}, scope ${scope}): ${chosen.question}`;
}

// Depth bands share maxIterations 50/35/15 by default (depths 0-1, 2, 3). A band's
// unspent share rolls down to the next band only once the band has no open node,
// and never up. When a band's spend reaches its share, its open nodes close as
// exhausted with reason budget, so the run stops waiting on work it cannot fund.
// An open node whose question an iteration already answered is marked answered
// first: the budget closes only work still outstanding, and an answered node
// never holds its band open against the roll-down.

function frontierScopeRefs(scope) {
  if (Array.isArray(scope)) return asStringList(scope);
  const single = normalizeText(scope);
  return single ? [single] : [];
}

// A node the store still holds open is search depth the run has not spent yet,
// so it belongs in the same debt ledger a leaf's own deferral lands in. Exhausted
// and blocked nodes stay frontier-only: their question is closed, and filing them
// as debt would report work the run is no longer allowed to do.
function mergeFrontierIntoSearchLedger(searchLedgerState, frontier) {
  const candidateCoverage = searchLedgerState.candidateCoverage;
  const debtById = new Map(
    (Array.isArray(searchLedgerState.searchDebt) ? searchLedgerState.searchDebt : [])
      .map((entry) => [entry.id, entry]),
  );
  for (const node of frontier.nodes) {
    const bugClass = `frontier:${node.nodeId}`;
    const iteration = node.iteration ?? 0;
    if (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active') {
      const id = `frontier-${node.nodeId}`;
      debtById.set(id, {
        id,
        iteration,
        targetRefs: frontierScopeRefs(node.scope),
        bugClass,
        disposition: 'deferred',
        rationale: `Frontier node ${node.nodeId} is still open: ${node.question}`,
        evidenceRefs: [`frontier-nodes.jsonl#${node.nodeId}`],
        reason: 'frontier node open',
        frontier: true,
      });
      addCandidateClass(candidateCoverage, bugClass, 'deferred', iteration);
    } else if (node.status === 'answered') {
      addCandidateClass(candidateCoverage, bugClass, 'covered', iteration);
    } else if (node.status === 'pruned' || node.status === 'abstained') {
      addCandidateClass(candidateCoverage, bugClass, 'ruled_out', iteration);
    }
  }
  searchLedgerState.searchDebt = [...debtById.values()]
    .sort((left, right) => left.iteration - right.iteration || left.id.localeCompare(right.id));
  for (const key of ['covered', 'ruledOut', 'deferred', 'blocked']) {
    candidateCoverage[key].sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));
  }
  for (const state of Object.values(candidateCoverage.byBugClass)) {
    state.iterations.sort((left, right) => Number(left) - Number(right));
  }
  return searchLedgerState;
}

 * Enforce record validation at the field level. parseJsonlDetailed only guards
 * JSON syntax; these warnings surface schema drift without changing the
 * reducer's default pass/fail behavior for currently-valid state.
 */

commit 33b6f92ac140ff1a2c5fc10aff35c7e96223e02f
Author: Michel Kerkmeester <82775228+MichelKerkmeester@users.noreply.github.com>
Date:   Wed Nov 18 01:32:46 2026 +0100

    fix(system-deep-loop): reduce the review frontier once after the seed
    
    Under the expansive depth mode the review loop binds its frontier node and
    dimension from the findings registry. The init step that creates that registry
    runs after the seed and writes no frontier, and nothing reduced before the
    loop, so iteration 1 had no frontier node and no frontier block. A single
    expansive-gated init step now runs the review reducer after the registry and
    strategy exist. A lifecycle test seeds a review packet, shows the registry has
    no frontier, runs the step and renders the block for the node it projected.
    
    Commit-Id: 0014845

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && rg -n -- '--depth-mode' .skilled/commands/deep/research.md .skilled/commands/deep/review.md | head; echo ---; rg -n '"depthMode"' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/assets/deep-research-config.json .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/assets/deep-review-config.json
.skilled/commands/deep/research.md:104:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 frontier nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as new questions under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), steers Next Focus to the shallowest open node, and turns a convergence stop into another iteration while frontier nodes stay open. It never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Fan-out lineages each get a share of the seeded nodes. |
.skilled/commands/deep/review.md:97:| `--depth-mode` | `default` \| `expansive` | `default`, the value when the flag is absent, runs exactly as before. `expansive` is opt-in: the run seeds depth-1 sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1 (record the pilot range SHA; merges are pruned as `merge-no-own-diff` and index rebuilds as `generated-artifact`), admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps (`expansiveDepth` in the config: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths), keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, holds open nodes as `searchDebt`, dispatches the active node's next uncovered dimension, and turns a review stop candidate into another iteration while nodes stay open. A node needs one iteration per configured dimension, so set `--max-iterations` to at least `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions` for the depth-1 band. It never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`. Fan-out lineages each get a share of the seeded nodes. |

31:This playbook provides 69 deterministic scenarios across 10 categories validating the current `deep-review` skill surface. The first 7 categories cover dimension/lifecycle and intra-routing review (50 scenarios). §15 covers command-flow stress tests (6 scenarios under CP-052..057), §16 covers the review-depth v2 rollout (7 scenarios under DRV-058..063 and DRV-075), and §17 covers fan-out dispatch (6 scenarios under DRV-064..067, DRV-071 and DRV-082). DRV-076 to DRV-082 cover the opt-in expansive depth flag for review. Default runs differ from the pre-change baseline only by the expansive-only agent paragraph, which is waived pending operator ratification. Each scenario maps to a dedicated feature file with the canonical objective, prompt summary, expected signals, and live source anchors.

181:> **Feature File:** [DRV-070](../manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/extend-completed-review-run.md)

194:> **Feature File:** [DRV-076](../manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md)

# deep-research: Manual Testing Playbook

> **EXECUTION POLICY**: Every scenario MUST be executed for real — not mocked, not stubbed, not classified as "unautomatable". AI agents executing these scenarios must run the actual commands, inspect real files, call real handlers, and verify real outputs. The only acceptable classifications are PASS, FAIL, or SKIP (with a specific sandbox blocker documented). "UNAUTOMATABLE" is not a valid status.

This document combines the manual testing contract for the `deep-research` skill into a single reference. The root playbook acts as the directory, review protocol, and orchestration guide while the per-feature files carry the scenario-specific execution truth.

> build · deepseek-v4.1-flash
[0m$ [0mcd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop && sed -n '320,425p' runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs
// 2. dedupe
  const key = dedupeKey(proposal.question);
  if (key === '') return 'dedupe';
  for (const node of nodes.values()) {
    // Any status counts: re-proposing a closed branch would reopen work the run
    // already settled, whether it was answered, exhausted or pruned.
    if (nodeDedupeKey(node) === key) return 'dedupe';
  }
  if (Array.isArray(knownTexts)) {
    for (const text of knownTexts) {
      if (typeof text === 'string' && dedupeKey(text) === key) return 'dedupe';
    }
  }

  // 3. depth
  const depth = depthFor(proposal, nodes);
  if (depth > caps.maxDepth) return 'depth';

  // 4. fan-out
  if (countLiveChildren(nodes, proposal.parentId) >= childCapFor(proposal.parentId, caps)) return 'fanout';
  if (countLiveNodes(nodes) >= caps.maxFrontierNodes) return 'fanout';

  // 5. budget
  const parent = proposal.parentId === 'root' ? null : nodes.get(proposal.parentId);
  if (Number.isFinite(proposal.budget) && parent && Number.isFinite(parent.budget)) {
    // Budgets only narrow from parent to child; a child that claims more than
    // its parent could spend work the run never allotted to that branch.
    if (proposal.budget > parent.budget) return 'budget';
  }

  // 6. cycle
  return checkCycle(proposal.parentId, nodes, caps);
}

function admitProposals({ artifactDir, iteration, proposals, knownTexts = [], target = 'research' }) {
  const mode = assertTarget(target);
  const storePath = path.join(artifactDir, NODE_STORE_FILE);
  const inboxPath = path.join(artifactDir, INBOX_FILE);
  const nodes = readNodeStore(storePath);
  const caps = readCaps(artifactDir, mode);
  // An unreadable review config must not silently widen the relevance check to
  // every path, so it degrades to the no-target object rather than being skipped.
  const reviewOptions = mode === 'review' ? { reviewConfig: readTargetConfig(artifactDir, mode) ?? {} } : {};
  const iterationValue = normalizeIteration(iteration);
  const admitted = [];
  const pruned = [];
  const list = Array.isArray(proposals) ? proposals : [];

  for (const proposal of list) {
    const accept = checkProposal(proposal, nodes, caps, knownTexts, reviewOptions);
    const depth = depthFor(proposal, nodes);
    const nodeId = `F-${depth}-${String(nodes.size + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`;
    const safe = isPlainObject(proposal) ? proposal : {};

    if (accept !== null) {
      const row = {
        nodeId,
        parentId: safe.parentId === undefined ? null : safe.parentId,
        depth,
        scope: safe.scope === undefined ? null : safe.scope,
        question: safe.question === undefined ? null : safe.question,
        status: 'pruned',
        reason: accept,
        iteration: iterationValue,
        dedupeKey: dedupeKey(safe.question),
      };
      appendJsonlRow(storePath, row);
      nodes.set(nodeId, row);
      pruned.push({ nodeId, reason: accept });
      continue;
    }

    const row = {
      nodeId,
      parentId: proposal.parentId,
      depth,
      scope: proposal.scope,
      question: proposal.question,
      status: 'open',
      reason: null,
      iteration: iterationValue,
      dedupeKey: dedupeKey(proposal.question),
    };
    appendJsonlRow(storePath, row);
    nodes.set(nodeId, row);
    // A review run has no question inbox: the store alone queues its nodes, and
    // an inbox row would add a counted question the review never asked.
    if (mode !== 'review') {
      appendJsonlRow(inboxPath, {
        id: nodeId,
        text: proposal.question,
        source: 'frontier',
        origin: 'frontier',
        injectedAtIteration: iterationValue,
      });
    }
    admitted.push(nodeId);
  }

  return { admitted, pruned };
}

function seedNodes({ artifactDir, iteration = 0, candidates, knownTexts = [], target = 'research' }) {
  // Every seeded node sits at depth 1 against the run's synthetic root: the seed
  // sees only the topic, so it cannot justify a deeper placement on its own.

#### Description

#### How It Works

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/guides/quick-reference.md:59:| `--depth-mode` | default | `expansive` grows a depth-capped frontier of follow-up questions and vetoes a convergence stop while nodes stay open |
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/convergence/convergence.md:105:`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which is set to `maxIterations` when the config is created. Both the mode and the block are locked against the optimizer. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/state/state-format.md:81:`research/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`. A default run never creates it. Rows are append-only and carry `{nodeId, parentId, depth, scope, question, status, reason, iteration, dedupeKey}`. The last row for a node wins, so a status change is one more row rather than an edit. `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs` is the only writer.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/convergence/convergence.md:68:`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a frontier of follow-up questions beside the questions seeded at init. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, `budgetSplit` 0.5/0.35/0.15 for depths 1, 2 and 3, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to `maxIterations`. An absent flag means `default`, which renders the same bytes as before; `research-depth-mode-golden.vitest` proves it.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/state/state-format.md:299:`review/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`; a default run never creates it. Rows are append-only and carry `{nodeId, parentId, depth, scope, question, status, reason, iteration, dedupeKey}`. The last row per `nodeId` wins, so a status change is one more row rather than an edit, and `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs --target review` is its only writer, for seed, admit and close.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:459:Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, set to `maxIterations` at config creation. A run without the flag keeps the default path and runs exactly as before, and both the mode and the caps are locked against the optimizer.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:411:Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of follow-up questions beside the key questions seeded at init. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 per depth, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to `maxIterations`. A run without the flag keeps the default path and renders the same bytes as before.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:421:On a fan-out run, `fanout-run.cjs --depth-mode expansive` deals the seeded open depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order across lineages, writes each lineage's `frontier-nodes.jsonl` and inbox rows before it spawns, binds `frontier_scope:` in the lineage prompt and records `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. `fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers, each node keeping its lineage label, and records a partition guard that names any dealt node no lineage reports.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/quick-reference.md:48:| `--depth-mode` | default | `expansive` seeds depth-1 sub-target nodes, reviews the active node's next uncovered dimension, and vetoes a stop while nodes stay open |
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/quick-reference.md:165:**Frontier veto:** Under `--depth-mode=expansive` an open or active frontier node turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the review floor.

function nextFocusSection(strategy: string): string {
  const match = strategy.match(/<!-- ANCHOR:next-focus -->([\s\S]*?)<!-- \/ANCHOR:next-focus -->/);
  return match ? match[1] : '';
}

// Temp paths differ per run, so each is replaced with a stable token before comparison.
function normalize(text: string, specFolder: string): string {
  return text.split(specFolder).join('<SPEC>');
}

// The lineage prompt embeds the agent persona verbatim. The persona's leaf
// contract carries one paragraph that applies only when an iteration prompt
// holds a FRONTIER block, which a default run never renders. That paragraph
// alone is removed before comparison; any other persona or prompt byte still
// has to match the baseline.
const EXPANSIVE_ONLY_PERSONA_PARAGRAPH =
  /\n\n\*\*Frontier proposals \(expansive depth runs only\)\.\*\*[^\n]*\n[^\n]*\nYou only propose\.[^\n]*/;

function withoutExpansiveOnlyPersona(prompt: string): string {
  return prompt.replace(EXPANSIVE_ONLY_PERSONA_PARAGRAPH, '');
}

function renderArtifacts(variant: Variant): Record<Artifact, string> {
  const specFolder = writeFixture(variant);
  const researchDir = join(specFolder, 'research');
  const config = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(researchDir, 'deep-research-config.json'), 'utf8'));
  const reduced = reduceResearchState(specFolder, { write: false });
  const registry = reduced.registry;
  const nextFocus = nextFocusSection(reduced.strategy);
  const openQuestions = Array.isArray(registry.openQuestions) ? registry.openQuestions : [];

  const promptPack = renderPromptPack(templatePath, {
    state_summary: 'Iteration 2 of 10',
    research_topic: config.topic,
    current_iteration: 3,
    max_iterations: config.maxIterations,
    max_tool_calls_per_iteration: 12,
    next_focus: nextFocus.trim(),
    remaining_questions_list: JSON.stringify(openQuestions),
    carried_forward_open_questions: '[None yet]',
    last_3_summaries: '[None yet]',
    pivot_lineage: '[None yet]',
    saturated_directions: '[None yet]',
    state_paths_config: join(researchDir, 'deep-research-config.json'),
    state_paths_state_log: join(researchDir, 'deep-research-state.jsonl'),
    state_paths_strategy: join(researchDir, 'deep-research-strategy.md'),
    state_paths_registry: join(researchDir, 'findings-registry.json'),
    state_paths_iteration_pattern: join(researchDir, 'iterations', 'iteration-{NNN}.md'),
    state_paths_delta_pattern: join(researchDir, 'deltas', 'iter-{NNN}.jsonl'),
    frontier_block: '',
  });

  const lineagePrompt = buildLoopPrompt(
    'research',
    '.opencode/specs/system-speckit/999-golden',
    '.opencode/specs/system-speckit/999-golden/research/lineages/alpha',
    'golden-session',
    { label: 'alpha', kind: 'cli-opencode', model: 'fixture/model', iterations: 3 },
    config.topic,
    { stopPolicy: 'convergence' },
  );

  const stateLogRows = readFileSync(join(researchDir, 'deep-research-state.jsonl'), 'utf8')
    .split('\n')
    .filter((line) => line.trim() !== '')
    .map((line) => JSON.parse(line));
  const decisionInput = buildStopDecisionInput(
    variant.convergenceArgs,
    'research',
    { config, registry, stateLogRows },
    { nodes: [], edges: [] },
  );

  return {
    'registry.json': normalize(`${JSON.stringify(registry, null, 2)}\n`, specFolder),
    'dashboard.md': normalize(reduced.dashboard, specFolder),
    'next-focus.txt': normalize(nextFocus, specFolder),
    'prompt-pack.md': normalize(promptPack, specFolder),
    // The lineage prompt resolves its write surface against the working directory,
    // so that prefix is tokenized and the baseline holds in any checkout.
    'lineage-prompt.txt': withoutExpansiveOnlyPersona(lineagePrompt.split(process.cwd()).join('<CWD>')),
    'stop-decision-config.json': `${JSON.stringify(decisionInput.config, null, 2)}\n`,
  };
}

describe('research default path stays byte-identical without --depth-mode=expansive', () => {

// A free-text leaf focus must not hide a required dimension the run has not covered yet.
function pickReviewBaseFocus(iterationFiles, registry) {
  const leafFocus = iterationFiles.map((iteration) => iteration.nextFocus).filter(Boolean).at(-1) || '';
  const uncovered = Object.keys(registry.dimensionCoverage).filter((dimension) => !registry.dimensionCoverage[dimension]);
  const leafKey = String(leafFocus).toLowerCase().trim();
  if (leafFocus && (uncovered.length === 0 || uncovered.some((dimension) => leafKey.includes(dimension)))) {
    return leafFocus;
  }
  return uncovered[0] || leafFocus || '[All dimensions covered]';
}

// The expansive frontier is the run's own queue, and the node it chose carries
// the dimension that node still owes, so a leaf's free-text focus cannot point
// the next iteration at work the frontier has already closed or reordered.
function pickReviewFrontierFocus(frontier) {
  if (!frontier || typeof frontier !== 'object' || !frontier.activeNodeId) return '';
  const nodes = Array.isArray(frontier.nodes) ? frontier.nodes : [];
  const node = nodes.find((candidate) => candidate && candidate.nodeId === frontier.activeNodeId);
  if (!node) return '';
  const scope = Array.isArray(node.scope) ? node.scope.join(', ') : (node.scope || 'unscoped');
  return `Frontier node ${node.nodeId} (depth ${node.depth}, scope ${scope}): ${node.question} - review the ${frontier.activeNextDimension} dimension`;
}

function updateStrategyContent(strategyContent, registry, iterationFiles, options = {}, iterationRecords = []) {
  // Early return when there is no strategy file to update. Empty content
  // cannot contain the machine-owned anchors and replaceAnchorSection would
  // correctly fail-close — but for that specific case we intentionally skip
  // rather than throw so bootstrap flows (no strategy yet) still work.
  if (!strategyContent) {
    return strategyContent;
  }

  const anchorOptions = { createMissing: Boolean(options.createMissingAnchors) };
  const severity = registry.findingsBySeverity;
  const runningFindings = [
    `- P0 (Blockers): ${severity.P0}`,
    `- P1 (Required): ${severity.P1}`,
    `- P2 (Suggestions): ${severity.P2}`,
    `- Resolved: ${registry.resolvedFindingsCount}`,
  ].join('\n');

  const completedDimensions = Object.keys(registry.dimensionCoverage)
    .filter((dimension) => registry.dimensionCoverage[dimension])
    .map((dimension) => `- [x] ${dimension}`)
    .join('\n') || '[None yet]';

  const remainingDimensions = Object.keys(registry.dimensionCoverage)
    .filter((dimension) => !registry.dimensionCoverage[dimension])
    .map((dimension) => `- [ ] ${dimension}`)
    .join('\n') || '[All dimensions complete]';

  // Default next-focus: a leaf focus naming an uncovered dimension, else the first uncovered dimension, else the leaf focus.
  let nextFocus = pickReviewBaseFocus(iterationFiles, registry);
  // An open node outranks the leaf's own focus; the pivot override and the
  // blocked-stop recovery below still outrank the node, because a saturated
  // direction or a hard block is what the run must heed before spending depth.
  const frontierFocus = pickReviewFrontierFocus(registry.frontier);
  if (frontierFocus) nextFocus = frontierFocus;
  if (registry.divergence.currentFocus) {
    nextFocus = registry.divergence.currentFocus;
  }

  // Prefer the latest blocked-stop
  // recovery only when blocked-stop is genuinely the most recent loop event.
  // The earlier implementation read timestamps from the markdown iteration

 `--convergence-mode` changes the stop path. `off` disables convergence STOP while the hard caps stay active. `sliding-window` scores novelty over a bounded window. `divergent` sends an eligible legal stop into one bounded pivot round instead of synthesis, then returns to the loop.
 
+`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. A seed step writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1, and the leaf may propose children only when its prompt carries a frontier block. An admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, and prunes the proposal on the first failure. Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first and writes it as a frontier line, so visiting a node does not read as answering it. While a node stays open the frontier vetoes a soft convergence stop, which never outranks the iteration cap or the other hard stops, and the caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.

+`fork` is reserved and deferred, so the runtime neither emits nor accepts it. `releaseReadinessState` starts at `in-progress`. It moves to `release-blocking` while any unresolved P0 remains. It moves to `converged` when the run stops converged with a PASS or CONDITIONAL verdict.
 
 ### Convergence and the Stop Gates
 
@@ -152,7 +153,9 @@ Convergence is a weighted vote over three signals.
 
 The weighted score has to reach `compositeStopScore`, which defaults to 0.60, before the loop may consider a stop. Clearing the threshold is not enough on its own. Nine binary gates have to pass as well, and a failure writes a `blocked_stop` event with `blockedBy` and a per-gate `gateResults` record. The gate list and its criteria live in `references/convergence/convergence.md`.

+`--depth-mode=expansive` is the one stop-path change that grows work instead of ending it. A seed step writes the depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, the active node's next uncovered dimension steers the iteration, and an admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, pruning the proposal on the first failure. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, which no default run creates, and an open node is filed as open search debt, so the run spends the iteration that node still needs. The frontier veto never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the floor above, and stuck recovery closes the active node as `exhausted`. The caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.

=== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/feature-catalog/council/divergent-pivot-seat-cap-and-retry.md
---
title: "Divergent pivot seat cap and council retry"
description: "A divergent pivot council runs at most three seats per attempt, and a retryable first-attempt quorum failure reopens the pivot once as attempt 2."
trigger_phrases:
  - "divergent pivot seat cap"
  - "council quorum retry"
  - "maxCouncilSeatOutputs"
  - "pivot attempt 2"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Divergent pivot seat cap and council retry

## 1. OVERVIEW

A divergent pivot runs a council of seats. The seat cap is three per council attempt. When the first attempt misses council quorum in a retryable way, the workflow reopens the pivot once as attempt 2, and that retry is the only re-entry.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Seat Cap

`maxCouncilSeatOutputs` defaults to 3 in `runtime/lib/deep-loop/divergent-pivot.ts`. The research and review pivot scripts scope the seat count to the current pivot id and attempt.

### One Retry

When finalize reports a retryable council quorum failure, the research and review command workflows reopen the pivot once as attempt 2. The skip condition stops any further retry, so attempt 2 never reopens.

### Attempt Identity

Seat event ids include the attempt number, so attempt 2 does not reuse attempt 1 event ids or seat paths. `runtime/scripts/divergent-pivot.cjs` carries the attempt number and the retry signal through its `prepare` and `finalize` subcommands, which is the seam the workflows call.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/divergent-pivot.ts` | Shared | Seat cap default, seat scope and retryable quorum result. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/divergent-pivot.cjs` | Script | Carries the attempt number and retry signal through `prepare` and `finalize`. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/divergent-research-pivot.ts` | Script | Scopes the research seat count to the pivot id and attempt. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/scripts/divergent-review-pivot.ts` | Script | Scopes the review seat count to the pivot id and attempt. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` | Command workflow | Reopens the pivot once as attempt 2 after a retryable quorum failure. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/integration/divergent-pivot.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Seat scope, the attempt-2 retry, and attempt identity cases. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/divergent-pivot-seat-scope.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Seat-count scope per pivot id and attempt. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/council/multi-seat-dispatch.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Multi-seat dispatch with any seat ids, unchanged by this behavior. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/manual-testing-playbook/council/divergent-council-quorum-retry.md` | Manual playbook | DLR-062: three seats per attempt and one retry after a first-attempt quorum miss. |

---

## 4. SOURCE METADATA

- Group: Council
- Canonical catalog source: `feature-catalog.md`
- Feature file path: `council/divergent-pivot-seat-cap-and-retry.md`

Related references:
- [multi-seat-dispatch.md](multi-seat-dispatch.md) - Multi-seat dispatch
=== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/feature-catalog/coverage-graph/source-quality-class.md
---
title: "Source quality class"
description: "Research SOURCE nodes carry a quality_class derived at the graph upsert from where the source lives, and the source-diversity signal reads that derived value."
trigger_phrases:
  - "source quality class"
  - "quality_class"
  - "source diversity quality"
  - "source node quality class"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Source quality class (quality_class)

## 1. OVERVIEW

The graph upsert sets `quality_class` on each research SOURCE node from where the source lives. A `quality_class` the leaf supplies is overwritten, so a leaf cannot raise source diversity by naming a class. The source-diversity signal reads the derived value.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Derivation

`runtime/scripts/upsert.cjs` derives the class from the source `path`, then `name`, then `id`, and writes it on research SOURCE nodes. The derived value replaces any leaf-supplied `quality_class`.

### Closed Vocabulary

`runtime/lib/coverage-graph/coverage-graph-query.ts` exports the frozen `SOURCE_QUALITY_CLASS` vocabulary: `repo-code`, `repo-test`, `repo-spec`, `skill-doc`, `ledger-state`, `external-doc`, `external-other` and `unclassified`.

### Diversity Signal

The source-diversity signal reads the derived class from each SOURCE node, not a value the leaf wrote.

---

=== .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/feature-catalog/executor/landed-iteration-no-redispatch.md
---
title: "Landed iteration is not redispatched"
description: "A research or review iteration that already landed is not dispatched a second time when its verify check fails with executor_missing, findings_not_enumerated or ledger_backing_missing."
trigger_phrases:
  - "landed iteration redispatch"
  - "redispatch once"
  - "executor_missing redispatch"
  - "findings_not_enumerated redispatch"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Landed iteration is not redispatched (redispatch_once)

## 1. OVERVIEW

When the mechanical leaf check fails, the workflow may re-run the iteration dispatch once. For three failure reasons the iteration has already landed: the narrative, state record and delta all exist, and a second leaf would refuse to overwrite them. For those reasons the workflow does not redispatch the iteration.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Redispatch Filter

The `redispatch_once` step in `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` and `deep-review.yaml` re-runs `step_dispatch_iteration` once for the same iteration number, then re-runs the check. It skips that redispatch when the failure reason is `executor_missing`, `findings_not_enumerated` or `ledger_backing_missing`.

### Outcome When Skipped

The iteration is treated as still failing, with no second dispatch. If the check still fails, the iteration is recorded as error and the loop continues. The workflow never dispatches a given iteration more than once.

### Verify Step Note

The verify step's `command_note` names the skipped reasons, so the written workflow and the redispatch filter agree.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` | Command workflow | The `redispatch_once` reason filter and the verify step's `command_note`. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` | Command workflow | The same reason filter for review iterations. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/verify-iteration.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Reason-table case and a YAML-reading assertion on both command files. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/deep-research-reduce-state.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Landed-plus-error-row fixture for research. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/deep-review-state-reducer.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Landed-plus-error-row fixture for review. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/manual-testing-playbook/executor/landed-iteration-skips-redispatch.md` | Manual playbook | DLR-063: a landed iteration is not dispatched a second time. |

---

## 4. SOURCE METADATA

- Group: Executor
- Canonical catalog source: `feature-catalog.md`
- Feature file path: `executor/landed-iteration-no-redispatch.md`

title: "Research Claude config dir"

# Research Claude config dir (--claude-config-dir)

## 1. OVERVIEW

A deep-research run on the `cli-claude-code` executor runs Claude under a configured config dir, the same way review does. The flag is optional for research, and an empty value means no config dir is set. Review still requires the flag on `cli-claude-code`, and every other executor refuses it.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Flag Validation

`runtime/scripts/dispatch-iteration.cjs` validates `--claude-config-dir` for both loop types. A review run on `cli-claude-code` without the flag fails with `missing required flag '--claude-config-dir'`. A research run accepts the flag. Any executor other than `cli-claude-code` refuses it with `flag '--claude-config-dir' is only valid with --executor cli-claude-code`.

### Command Forwarding

The `if_cli_claude_code` branch of `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` forwards the configured dir to the dispatch command with a render hint. A contract test pins that branch in both command YAMLs, so a one-sided edit fails the suite.

### Audit Record

The executor audit row for a research iteration carries the configured dir, so an operator can read which Claude config an iteration used from the audit alone.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/dispatch-iteration.cjs` | Script | Validates the flag per loop type and passes the dir into the Claude run. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` | Command workflow | The `if_cli_claude_code` branch forwards the configured dir. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/executor-audit.ts` | Shared | Reads `executor.configDir` into the audit row. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/dispatch-iteration.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Flag refusal and acceptance cases for research and review. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/executor-audit-cli-branch-receipts.test.ts` | Node test | Research twin of the claude-code branch receipt; the audit row carries the configured dir. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/executor-branch-contract.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Pins the `if_cli_claude_code` branch in both command YAMLs. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/manual-testing-playbook/executor/research-claude-config-dir.md` | Manual playbook | DLR-064: research on `cli-claude-code` runs under the configured Claude config dir. |

---

## 4. SOURCE METADATA

- Group: Executor
- Canonical catalog source: `feature-catalog.md`
- Feature file path: `executor/research-claude-config-dir.md`

# Fan-out review coverage merge

## 1. OVERVIEW

A review fan-out runs its lineages in parallel and merges their registries into one. The merge carries three coverage surfaces forward from the lineages: candidate coverage, dimension coverage and search coverage.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Merged Surfaces

`runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` `mergeReviewRegistries` merges each lineage's candidate coverage, dimension coverage and search coverage, and adds the merged surfaces to the returned registry.

### Merge Behavior

The surfaces are combined across lineages rather than read from one lineage alone, so the merged registry reflects coverage from every lineage in the fan-out.

---

title: "Research prompt pack contract"
description: "The research prompt pack lists a conditional allowed write for the run's research/research.md and names the Recommended Next Focus heading the reducer parses."
trigger_phrases:
  - "research prompt pack contract"
  - "research.md allowed write"
  - "Recommended Next Focus heading"
  - "research prompt pack"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Research prompt pack contract

## 1. OVERVIEW

The research prompt pack is the rendered per-iteration instruction set for research runs. It matches the research agent definition on two points: a conditional allowed write for the run's `research/research.md`, and the `Recommended Next Focus` heading that the reducer parses to choose the next focus.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Conditional research.md Write

The pack's allowed-write list carries the run's `research/research.md` as one conditional entry, worded relative to the `iterations/` directory. The pack renders the entry the same way the agent definition states the rule.

### Recommended Next Focus Heading

The pack names the `Recommended Next Focus` heading, the heading the reducer reads from the latest leaf when it derives the next focus. The heading is part of the contract the reducer depends on.

### Rendering

`runtime/lib/deep-loop/prompt-pack.ts` renders the pack by plain token substitution and throws on a missing variable. The conditional entry is carried in the template text, so the command YAMLs need no new render variable.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/assets/prompt-pack-iteration.md.tmpl` | Template | Carries the conditional allowed-write entry for `research.md`. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/prompt-pack.ts` | Shared | Renders the pack by token substitution. |
| `.skilled/agents/deep-research.md` | Agent contract | The rule the rendered pack must match. Checked, not edited by this change. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/prompt-pack.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Rendered-pack case for the research write entry and the agent and pack lockstep case. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/manual-testing-playbook/prompt-rendering/research-prompt-pack-contract.md` | Manual playbook | DLR-060: the conditional research.md write path and the Recommended Next Focus heading. |

---

## 4. SOURCE METADATA

- Group: Prompt rendering
- Canonical catalog source: `feature-catalog.md`
- Feature file path: `prompt-rendering/research-prompt-pack-contract.md`

Related references:
- [prompt-pack.md](prompt-pack.md) - Prompt pack rendering

# Completed-run extension (--extend)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--extend=N` reopens a completed research run in the same session and generation and adds `N` iterations. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event, and keeps the prior synthesis as `research/synthesis-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md`. `--extend-minutes=M` adds minutes to the duration budget of the same extension.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Lineage And Preconditions

The extension uses lineage mode `completed-continue`. The run must already be complete. An unfinished run continues with `--lineage-mode=resume` instead. The extension is refused with fan-out.

### Iteration And Duration Budget

The iteration cap rises by `N` over the prior count. `--extend-minutes=M` is valid only with `--extend`. The new duration budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts time since `createdAt`, so an extension started after the old budget still runs.

### Stop Interaction

Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new can stop again at once on `all_questions_answered`. Pass `--convergence-mode=off`, use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`, or add questions to `research/inbox.jsonl`.

### Ledger And Reducer Routing

The `run_extended` stem is part of the deep-research ledger schema, and the deep-research reducer routes it. The lifecycle event filters in `deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` read the same event.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/commands/deep/research.md` | Command doc | Flag rows for `--extend` and `--extend-minutes`. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` | Command workflow | Lineage handling and the snapshot of the prior synthesis. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-research-ledger-schema/deep-research-ledger-schema.ts` | Shared | Defines the `run_extended` stem and its payload. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-research-reducers/deep-research-reducer.ts` | Shared | Routes the `run_extended` stem. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` | Script | Lifecycle event filters that read the extension event. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|

| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/extend-completed-research-run.md` | Manual playbook | DR-065: same-session continuation, the `run_extended` event and the synthesis snapshot. |

# Completed-run extension (--extend)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--extend=N` reopens a completed review run in the same session and generation and adds `N` iterations. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event, and keeps the prior review report as `review/review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` before re-synthesis. `--extend-minutes=M` adds minutes to the duration budget of the same extension.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Lineage And Preconditions

The extension uses lineage mode `completed-continue`. The run must already be complete. An unfinished review continues with `--lineage-mode=resume` instead. The extension is refused with fan-out.

### Iteration And Duration Budget

The iteration cap rises by `N` over the prior count. `--extend-minutes=M` is valid only with `--extend`. The new duration budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts time since `createdAt`.

### Stop Interaction

Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new can stop again at once on `all_dimensions_clean`. Pass `--convergence-mode=off` or use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`.

### Ledger And Reducer Routing

The `run_extended` stem is part of the deep-review ledger schema, and the deep-review reducer routes it. `runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` reads the extension through its live lineage modes.

title: "Next Focus free-text guard"
description: "Deep-research Next Focus skips answered questions and status sentences and reads the latest leaf's Recommended Next Focus, so free text cannot hijack the next focus."

# Next Focus free-text guard

## 1. OVERVIEW

The research next focus comes from the carried-forward open questions and the latest leaf. Answered questions and status sentences are dropped from the carried-forward list, and the focus reads the leaf's `Recommended Next Focus`. Free text in a status line does not replace the focus.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Carried-Forward Questions

`buildCarriedForwardOpenQuestions` in `runtime/lib/deep-loop/continuity-thread.cjs` drops answered keys and applies a question-shape rule, so a status sentence is not carried forward as an open question.

### Focus Derivation

`deriveNextFocusFromContinuity` in the same file derives the focus from the continuity thread. `resolveNextFocus` in `deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` derives it from the latest leaf's `Recommended Next Focus`, and the registry build passes the answered and resolved question set into the carried-forward list.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/continuity-thread.cjs` | Shared | Carried-forward question filter and continuity-thread focus derivation. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` | Script | Next Focus resolution from the latest leaf and the answered-question input to the registry. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/continuity-thread.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Order, question-shape and answered-filter cases. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/deep-research-reduce-state.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Answered-filter, stale-status-sentence and leaf-reader cases. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/iteration-execution-and-state-discipline/next-focus-ignores-answered-free-text.md` | Manual playbook | DR-066: Next Focus skips answered free text and status sentences. |

title: "Next Focus free-text guard"

# Next Focus free-text guard

## 1. OVERVIEW

Review Next Focus is the next dimension the review audits. When the latest leaf's `nextFocus` names no dimension, the first uncovered dimension wins, so free text does not hijack the review focus. The strategy file and the dashboard apply the same rule.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Strategy And Dashboard

`runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` sets the review base pick in the strategy focus block and in its dashboard twin. An uncovered dimension outranks a leaf `nextFocus` that names no dimension.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` | Script | The strategy focus block and the dashboard Next Focus, which pick the first uncovered dimension. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/deep-review-state-reducer.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Review twin case asserting the strategy and dashboard Next Focus. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/iteration-execution-and-state-discipline/review-next-focus-first-uncovered-dimension.md` | Manual playbook | DRV-073: Review Next Focus names the first uncovered dimension. |

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '2535,2610p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs
function resolveNextFocus(registry, iterationFiles, iterationRecords) {
  const rejectedIndex = registry.rejectedPatternIndex;
  const latestBlockedStop = Array.isArray(registry.blockedStopHistory)
    ? registry.blockedStopHistory.at(-1)
    : null;
  const latestIteration = Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords.at(-1) : null;

  if (registry.divergence?.currentFocus
    && isFiniteNumber(registry.divergence.activeSourceIteration)
    && registry.divergence.activeSourceIteration > (readIterationNumber(latestIteration) ?? 0)) {
    return registry.divergence.currentFocus;
  }

  if (latestBlockedStop) {
    const latestBlockedTimestamp = getTimestampValue(latestBlockedStop.timestamp);
    const latestIterationTimestamp = getTimestampValue(latestIteration?.timestamp);

    if (latestBlockedTimestamp > latestIterationTimestamp) {
      const recoveryStrategy = latestBlockedStop.recoveryStrategy || '[No recovery strategy recorded]';
      const recoveryMatch = findRejectedPatternMatch(
        { text: recoveryStrategy, category: 'recovery' },
        rejectedIndex,
        { category: 'recovery' },
      );
      if (!recoveryMatch) {
        return [
          `BLOCKED on: ${formatBlockedByList(latestBlockedStop.blockedBy)}`,
          `Recovery: ${recoveryStrategy}`,
          'Address the blocking gates before the next iteration.',
        ].join('\n');
      }
    }
  }

  // Under expansive depth mode an open frontier node outranks the continuity
  // focus; the pivot focus and blocked-stop recovery above still come first.
  if (registry.frontier) {
    const frontierFocus = pickFrontierFocus(registry.frontier, iterationRecords);
    if (frontierFocus) return frontierFocus;
  }

  const filteredInputs = filterNextFocusInputs(registry, iterationFiles, iterationRecords);
  // A leaf recommendation goes through the same rejected-pattern suppression as every other next-focus candidate.
  const latestLeafNextFocus = [...(Array.isArray(filteredInputs.iterationFiles) ? filteredInputs.iterationFiles : [])]
    .sort((left, right) => (left.run || 0) - (right.run || 0))
    .map((iteration) => iteration.nextFocus)
    .filter((value) => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim())
    .filter((value) => !findRejectedPatternMatch({ text: value, category: 'next-focus' }, rejectedIndex, { category: 'next-focus' }))
    .at(-1) || '';
  const continuityFocus = deriveNextFocusFromContinuity({
    iterationFiles: filteredInputs.iterationFiles,
    iterationRecords: filteredInputs.iterationRecords,
    carriedForwardOpenQuestions: filteredInputs.carriedForwardOpenQuestions,
    machineOpenQuestions: filteredInputs.openQuestions,
    leafNextFocus: latestLeafNextFocus,
    resolvedQuestions: Array.isArray(registry.resolvedQuestions) ? registry.resolvedQuestions : [],
  });

  const normalizedFocus = normalizeOptionalText(continuityFocus).toLowerCase();
  const shouldUseIdeaFocus = !normalizedFocus
    || normalizedFocus === '[none yet]'
    || normalizedFocus === '[all tracked questions are resolved]';
  if (!shouldUseIdeaFocus) {
    return continuityFocus;
  }

  const promotedIdea = Array.isArray(registry.promotedIdeas)
    ? registry.promotedIdeas[0]
    : null;
  return promotedIdea?.idea || continuityFocus;
}

220:In a fan-out run the cross-lineage merge is the authoritative rollup, where any active P0, or any lineage's required quality gate still failing at its stop, becomes a merged FAIL.
231:The stuck count and the consecutive failure count are rebuilt from the state log on every pass, so a resumed run counts the same as one that never paused. Do not create a context-loop report or widen scope beyond the declared target.

### State Packet Location

The review state packet always lives under the target spec's local `review/` folder.
Root-spec targets use `{spec_folder}/review/` directly.
Child-phase and sub-phase targets use **flat-first** placement: the first run writes flat, and a `pt-NN` sibling packet is allocated only when prior content already exists for a different target.
Full resolution rule and worked examples: `references/state/state-format.md` §1.

Core artifacts: `deep-review-config.json`, `deep-review-state.jsonl`, `deep-review-strategy.md`, `deep-review-dashboard.md`, `.deep-review-pause`, `resource-map.md`, `review-report.md`, and `iterations/iteration-NNN.md`.

The loop writes `review/review-report.md` itself at synthesis rather than scaffolding it through `create.sh --level review`.
The report is assembled from the findings registry and the JSONL state, so its content exists only once the loop has run, and a scaffolded skeleton would be overwritten whole.
`create.sh --level review` exists for a review packet authored by hand, and its template carries the same nine sections the loop emits.

### Core Innovation: Fresh Context Per Iteration

Each agent dispatch gets a fresh context window; state continuity comes from files, not memory, preventing accumulated findings from biasing later dimensions. Init writes config/strategy/JSONL; each loop reads state, checks convergence, dispatches one dimension, records findings, and reduces state; synthesis compiles the report and saves continuity.

### Review Dimensions

The four primary review dimensions (configured in `assets/review-mode-contract.yaml`):

| Dimension | Focus | Key Questions |
|-----------|-------|---------------|
| **Correctness** | Logic, behavior, error handling | Behavior matches claims? Edge cases handled? |
| **Security** | Vulnerabilities, exposure, trust boundaries | Inputs validated? Credentials exposed? |
| **Spec-Alignment / Traceability** | Spec vs. implementation fidelity | Code matches spec.md? Planned items present? |
| **Completeness / Maintainability** | Coverage, dead code, documentation | TODOs resolved? Code self-documenting? |

### Lifecycle + Reducer Contract

Review mode is lineage-aware.

- **Lifecycle modes:** `new`, `resume`, and `restart`.
- **Required lineage fields:** `sessionId`, `parentSessionId`, `lineageMode`, `generation`, `continuedFromRun`, and `releaseReadinessState`.
- **Reducer contract:** consumes the latest JSONL delta, the new iteration file, and prior reduced state, then emits finding registry, dashboard metrics, and strategy updates.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:395:**Failure semantics**: when any new P0/P1 finding is missing a packet or a required field, the workflow records `{"event":"claim_adjudication","passed":false,"missingPackets":[...]}` in `deep-review-state.jsonl`. On the next loop, `step_check_convergence` step 0 (universal pre-check) routes STOP to `BLOCKED` with `blockedBy: ["claimAdjudicationGate"]` until a follow-up iteration rewrites the packet. Downgraded findings have their `finalSeverity` updated. The original severity is preserved in the iteration file for audit trail.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md:199:A: `resume` keeps the same `sessionId` and appends a `resumed` event. `restart` archives the current `research/` tree under `research_archive/`, mints a fresh `sessionId`, increments `generation` and appends a `restarted` event. `completed-continue` comes from `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session, raises the cap to the runs so far plus N, snapshots the prior synthesis and appends an `extended` event.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/SKILL.md:135:`completed-continue` is set by `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session and generation, raises the iteration cap to the runs so far plus N, and appends a typed `run_extended` event.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/quick-reference.md:108:| `completed-continue` | `--extend=N`: snapshot the completed review report as `review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md` and reopen the run for N more iterations in the same session |
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/README.md:141:The lineage block carries `sessionId`, `parentSessionId`, `lineageMode`, `generation`, `continuedFromRun` and `releaseReadinessState`. A first run is mode `new`. `resume` continues the same lineage without changing generation. `restart` opens a new generation and archives the prior packet. `completed-continue` is set by `--extend=N`, which reopens a completed run in the same session and generation and records `continuedFromRun`.

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODULE: Optimizer Manifest Anti-Convergence Contract
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. IMPORTS
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. TYPES
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

type ManifestField = {
  name: string;
};

type InvariantConstraint = {
  name: string;
  type: string;
  left: string;
  right: string;
  message: string;
};

type InvariantGroup = {
  key: string;
  products: string[];
  rejectBeforeScoring: boolean;
  lockedFields: string[];
  fieldAliases: Record<string, string[]>;
  constraints: InvariantConstraint[];
};

type OptimizerManifest = {
  tunableFields: ManifestField[];
  lockedFields: ManifestField[];
  invariantGroups: InvariantGroup[];
};

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. CONSTANTS
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const testDir = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const repoRoot = resolve(testDir, '..', '..', '..', '..', '..');
const manifestPath = resolve(
  repoRoot,
  'skills',
  'system-spec-kit',
  'runtime',
  'cli',
  'optimizer',
  'optimizer-manifest.json',
);
const reviewConfigPath = resolve(
  repoRoot,
  'skills',
  'system-deep-loop',
  'deep-review',
  'assets',
  'deep-review-config.json',
);

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 4. HELPERS
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function readManifest(): OptimizerManifest {
  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, 'utf8')) as OptimizerManifest;
}

function readPath(source: Record<string, unknown>, dottedPath: string): unknown {
  return dottedPath.split('.').reduce<unknown>((current, key) => {
    if (!current || typeof current !== 'object' || Array.isArray(current)) return undefined;
