[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '120,142p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/SKILL.md
The gateway authorizes, fences, and receipts the write, then refreshes `{state_paths.state_log}` from the ledger. Do not write that file directly.
3. Respect the LEAF-agent constraint: no sub-dispatch, no nested loops, and no more tool calls per iteration than `config.maxToolCallsPerIteration`.

Failure modes include `iteration_file_missing`, `iteration_file_empty`, `jsonl_not_appended`, `jsonl_missing_fields`, `jsonl_parse_error` and `executor_missing`, which fails a non-native iteration whose state log or lineage metadata names no executor. Three trailing failed iterations stop the loop with stop reason `error`.

### Lifecycle Contract

Runtime-supported lifecycle modes:

| Mode | Meaning |
|------|---------|
| `new` | First run against the spec folder |
| `resume` | Continue the active lineage and append a typed `resumed` JSONL event |
| `restart` | Archive the existing research tree, mint a fresh `sessionId`, increment `generation`, and append a typed `restarted` event |

`completed-continue` is set by `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session and generation, raises the iteration cap to the runs so far plus N, and appends a typed `run_extended` event.

The deferred mode `fork` is reserved but not runtime-supported.

### Code-Graph Readiness TrustState Surface

The live code-graph readiness contract reaches four TrustState values: `live`, `stale`, `absent`, and `unavailable`. `cached`, `imported`, `rebuilt`, and `rehomed` remain declared in the shared TrustState type for compatibility, but the readiness helpers used here do not emit them today.

---

| Mode | Live Behavior |
|------|---------------|
| `default` | Uses normal full-history convergence signals and sends a legal STOP to synthesis and verdict derivation. This is the fallback when no mode is supplied. |
| `off` | Is accepted and persisted, but the shipped review YAML has no `off` decision branch; review therefore follows the same legal-stop and synthesis path as `default`. Use `stopPolicy: "max-iterations"` when convergence must remain telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling. |
| `sliding-window` | Is accepted and persisted, but windowed graph-novelty telemetry is research-only in the shared runtime; review therefore retains its ordinary full-history signals, legal-stop gates, and synthesis path. |
| `divergent` | After all review gates pass, translates the eligible `all_dimensions_clean` STOP into a read-only three-seat direction-selection pivot and continues with the selected review direction. |

#### Expansive Depth Mode

`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which is set to `maxIterations` when the config is created. Both the mode and the block are locked against the optimizer. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.

After the review floor, an expansive run that still holds an open or active node turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`, whichever candidate it was, `all_dimensions_clean` or a composite convergence vote. The veto never creates a stop of its own and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the `minIterations` floor. Under stuck recovery the handler closes the active node as `exhausted` with reason `stuck`, so a node the run cannot fund stops holding the veto open.

#### Divergent Pivot Contract

Review has one eligible reason: the exact internal reason `all_dimensions_clean` with decision `STOP`. The nine legal-stop gates run before the divergent branch and remain unchanged. `maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `stuckRecovery`, `error`, `manualStop`, `userPaused` and `maxDurationReached` are explicitly excluded. Max iterations, pause or cancellation, manual stop, unrecoverable error, a reached duration limit and any blocked security or quality gate therefore never dispatch a pivot Council.

Each eligible pivot runs one native, in-process Council round with exactly three distinct seats: analytical, critical, and pragmatic. If that round misses quorum, because a seat fails to return or returns a report that does not parse, the pivot reopens once as attempt 2 with three fresh seat outputs. A second quorum miss, or three valid seats that do not agree, fails the pivot closed. `maxCouncilSeatOutputs` caps one council attempt at 3 seat outputs, so a pivot records at most 6. It does not use an external CLI. The transaction requires all three seat returns to be parse-valid and at least two seats to materially endorse the same candidate without a high-severity blocker. Candidate sources are persisted unswept dimensions, search debt, producer-consumer gaps, negative-test gaps, and traceability gaps. Candidate generation rejects directions without in-scope evidence and directions that imply implementation, a fix, mutation, writing, or target expansion.

The guarantees are absolute: divergent mode never changes the PASS/CONDITIONAL/FAIL mapping, never authorizes a fix, and never makes the review target writable. A successful pivot returns to the loop before `phase_synthesis`; verdict computation exists only inside `phase_synthesis`, where an active P0 or a required quality gate still failing at the terminal stop yields FAIL, active P1 or open search debt yields CONDITIONAL, and no active P0, active P1 or open search debt yields PASS. A failed pivot fails closed to that existing synthesis path.

104:| `--extend` | positive integer, as `--extend=N` | Extends a completed run in place by `N` iterations, lineage mode `completed-continue`. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event and keeps the prior review report as `review/review-report-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md`. The run must be complete, so use `--lineage-mode=resume` for an unfinished one. Refused with fan-out. Under `stopPolicy: convergence` an extension with nothing new can re-stop on `all_dimensions_clean` at once: pass `--convergence-mode=off` or use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`. |
105:| `--extend-minutes` | positive integer, as `--extend-minutes=M` | Minutes added to the duration budget on an extension, default the prior `maxDurationMinutes`. The new budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts idle time since `createdAt`, so an extension started after the old budget still runs. Valid only with `--extend`. |

SC-002 admission half met: 4 seed depth-1 nodes (rooted at prompt-policy.ts and the default prompt-policy JSON), 8 admitted beyond them (5 at iteration 2, 3 at iteration 3), scorer at depth 2 (F-2-005).

A run without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` turns on a research frontier: the run seeds depth-one nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as nodes under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps, and holds a convergence stop while frontier nodes stay open. The live pilot runs are still open, so no pilot result is recorded here.

A review without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under the same caps as research, keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, and holds open nodes as `searchDebt`. The live pilot runs that would show this mode on real targets are still open, so this page describes the shipped code and its tests and records no pilot result.

# v2.1.6.0, Research Runs Use the Configured Claude Config Dir

A research run on Claude ignored the config dir the operator set. It ran under whichever account the shell had. Research now honors the configured dir.

&nbsp;

## What's New at a Glance

- Research accepts `--claude-config-dir` on `cli-claude-code` runs.
- The dir is checked before the prompt is read. A dir outside the allowed roots is refused.
- Claude runs under the configured dir. It overrides an inherited `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`.
- The audit record names the dir the run used.
- A run that names no dir behaves as before.

&nbsp;

## How It Was Checked

New cases cover the flag, its validation and the audit record. All ten dispatch cases pass in a detached run. Inside a Claude session, two skip under the Claude ancestry guard.

&nbsp;

## Upgrade

No migration required. Set `configDir` in the run's executor config to pin the account. An inherited `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` still reaches a research run that names no dir. Stripping it would change every default run, so it stays as it is.

&nbsp;

> Spec folder: `specs/system-deep-loop/loop-architecture/002-graph-engineering/007-expansive-depth-mode/002-research-claude-config-dir`
====

+Lineage handling is narrower than some older drafts. The runtime supports `new`, `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue`. Resume keeps the same session identifier and appends a typed `resumed` event. Restart archives the old research tree, mints a fresh session identifier, increments generation, and appends a typed `restarted` event. `completed-continue` is live through `--extend=N` on a completed run: it keeps the session identifier and generation, raises `maxIterations` by N, appends a typed `run_extended` event, and copies the prior synthesis to a versioned snapshot before the first new iteration.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:469:On a fan-out run the depth-1 nodes are dealt round-robin in node-id order, `frontier_scope:` binds each lineage's node ids beside `review_focus:` in its prompt, and the partition is recorded as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/README.md:158:`--depth-mode=expansive` is the one stop-path change that grows work instead of ending it. A seed step writes the depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, the active node's next uncovered dimension steers the iteration, and an admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, pruning the proposal on the first failure. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, which no default run creates, and an open node is filed as open search debt, so the run spends the iteration that node still needs. The frontier veto never outranks the iteration cap, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the floor above, and stuck recovery closes the active node as `exhausted`. The caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.
